14:I[8669,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DateSidebar"]
15:I[44692,["/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/08cykig6vtx_p.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/07qi2okp-.yp9.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0frujd.eyhz4o.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0n4z.7yu76je-.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/09-o.77-p0p36.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/1304s0wcp2kce.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/0j1uy9t~zxcf0.js","/paper-daily-site/_next/static/chunks/14_5o-nbg8k_r.js"],"DailyView"]
16:T43f,The core idea is to transform gradients into a near-isotropic coordinate system before quantization, so that uniform FP8 scaling introduces comparable error magnitudes across dimensions. The implementation has three parts: 1) Error diagnosis: Fisher information matrix whitening maps gradients onto a Riemannian manifold where the gradient distribution becomes approximately isotropic, with substantially lower quantization error than in Euclidean space. 2) Geometry approximation: K-FAC is used for Kronecker-factored approximation of the Fisher matrix, but only to construct temporary communication coordinates; the optimizer (Muon) update rule remains unchanged. 3) Selective application: Per-layer quantization sensitivity is first evaluated. K-FAC forward and inverse transforms are applied only to quantization-sensitive layers; all other layers use standard layer-wise Euclidean scaling to keep extra compute bounded. The entire pipeline only alters the coordinate representation during gradient communication and does not affect optimizer state storage or model weight precision.17:Tbb0,方法上，Co-LMLM 是一个 decoder-only Transformer，同时承担 LM 和 dense retriever 两个角色。核心设计有四部分。

第一，连续查询检索。模型在自回归生成时，如果输出特殊 token <FACT>，就读取该位置最后一层 hidden state 作为查询向量，而不是再生成一串自然语言查询。这个向量去检索外部索引中的 top-1 key，对应返回一个文本 fact span。系统把这个 span 直接插回上下文，形式是 <FACT> retrieved text </FACT>，然后从 </FACT> 之后继续解码。这样每次检索只额外消耗一个生成步，而不是多 token query decoding。这里的关键不是“把 hidden state 拿来做检索”本身，而是把检索动作内生进 next-token pretraining 流程里，让查询表示由 LM hidden state 自然承载。

第二，KB 形式从 relational tuples 改为 dense key-value store。value 是人类可读的文本 span，保留可编辑和可归因；key 是模型学出来的连续向量，避免关系模板约束。这样外置对象不再要求是显式关系三元组，只要能在文本中标出一个事实片段，就可以进入 KB。

第三，联合训练目标。训练数据中，原始文本的某些 factual spans 被 <FACT> 和 </FACT> 包起来。模型一方面照常做 next-token prediction，另一方面要学会在需要这些 span 时发出能命中对应 value 的查询向量。为此作者引入对比学习式 retrieval loss：每个 factual span 配一个自然语言问题，问题编码得到 query 侧表示，文档中的事实 span 提供正样本，其他 span 构成负样本。论文强调，预处理只规定“这里应该检索什么文本”，而不规定“查询必须长成什么样”，这和 Rel-LMLM 直接合成关系查询文本是根本区别。模型因此可以自己形成更灵活的检索几何。

第四，事实标注与数据构造流水线。为了摆脱 Wikipedia 的实体中心结构，作者设计了一个 annotation pipeline，在自由文本中标出 factual spans，并为每个 span 生成一个以该 span 为答案的自然语言问题。这样训练时既有语言模型上下文，也有 retrieval supervision。这个设计很关键，因为 Co-LMLM 的可扩展性不只来自连续查询，还来自“监督信号不再依赖结构化知识图谱”。从范式上看，它更像是在原始预训练语料上合成 retrieval-aware masking/slotting，再用 contrastive objective 把 slot 与外部 memory 对齐。

和现有方法相比，差异很清楚。它不同于标准 RAG，后者通常在预训练后外挂检索器，LM 本体并未被训练成主动外置知识；它也不同于 Rel-LMLM，后者的查询是离散文本、知识是关系型 tuple、监督路径是预先写死的；它也不同于纯 dense retrieval LM，因为这里返回的是可读文本片段，并直接进入生成上下文，目标是把 factual memory 从参数中迁出，而不是只做文档增强。18:T453,The paper addresses a concrete bottleneck in the Limited Memory Language Model line: how to move from a relational, Wikipedia-dependent, query-template-driven external memory setup to a more scalable pretraining paradigm. Rel-LMLM externalizes knowledge into a human-readable KB, but it is constrained in three ways. First, only facts that can be cast into relational tuples are easy to externalize. Second, retrieval is expressed as discrete natural-language queries, which adds decoding cost and limits expressivity. Third, the preprocessing pipeline effectively hard-codes retrieval pathways by synthesizing the exact query form in advance. Co-LMLM aims to keep the attractive properties of LMLMs—editable memory, attribution, direct unlearning via database operations—while replacing discrete relational queries with continuous vector queries and replacing relational tuples with dense-key/text-value memory. The broader goal is to train a model that learns, during pretraining, when to externalize knowledge and how to retrieve it, rather than merely learning to imitate a fixed retrieval template.19:Tb9b,Co-LMLM is a decoder-only Transformer trained to function as both a language model and a dense retriever. The method has four central pieces.

First, retrieval is triggered by a continuous query. During autoregressive decoding, when the model emits a special <FACT> token, the last-layer hidden state at that position is read out as the retrieval query. This vector is used to search an external index, and the top-1 retrieved textual fact span is inserted back into the context as <FACT> retrieved text </FACT>. Decoding then resumes after </FACT>. Each retrieval therefore costs only one extra autoregressive step, rather than requiring the model to generate a multi-token textual query. The key idea is not just using hidden states for retrieval, but integrating retrieval into the standard LM decoding loop.

Second, the KB is a dense key-value store rather than a relational database. Keys are learned continuous vectors; values are human-readable text spans. This preserves editability and attribution while removing the representational constraints of subject-relation-object schemas. Any factual span that can be marked in text can, in principle, be externalized.

Third, the model is trained with a joint LM-plus-retrieval objective. Training documents contain factual spans bracketed by <FACT> and </FACT>. The model is trained to predict tokens as usual, but also to emit query vectors that retrieve the correct span when needed. Retrieval supervision is provided through a contrastive objective built from question-span pairs: each factual span is paired with a natural-language question whose answer is that span, giving positives and negatives for dense retrieval training. A crucial design choice is that preprocessing specifies only when retrieval should happen and what text should be returned, not the exact textual form of the query. This is the main departure from Rel-LMLM, where the query representation is explicitly synthesized.

Fourth, the paper introduces an annotation pipeline for free-form corpora. Instead of relying on Wikipedia’s entity-centric structure, it marks factual spans in raw text and generates a natural-language question for each span. This creates retrieval-aware pretraining data without requiring a structured knowledge graph. In effect, the pipeline turns raw pretraining text into a retrieval-supervised corpus where factual spans are slotted out of the parametric channel and aligned with external memory.

Relative to prior work, the distinctions are clear. It is not standard RAG, because retrieval is not bolted on after pretraining; the model is pretrained to externalize knowledge. It is not Rel-LMLM, because queries are continuous rather than textual and the KB is not relational. It is also not just a dense retriever attached to an LM, because the retrieved object is a human-readable fact span that becomes part of the generation context, with the explicit goal of shifting factual memory out of the weights.1a:T821,The empirical story has three main parts.

First, Co-LMLM improves language modeling and factuality at the same time. Across model scales, it achieves lower perplexity than standard non-externalizing baselines and also outperforms comparable Rel-LMLM variants. At the same time, it substantially improves factual precision. The headline number in the paper is that at 360M parameters, Co-LMLM attains lower perplexity than HF SmolLM2-360M despite SmolLM2 being pretrained on roughly 40 times more data. That is not a perfectly controlled recipe match, but it is still a strong signal for data efficiency.

Second, factual QA performance is unusually strong for the scale. The paper reports that the 360M Co-LMLM reaches SimpleQA-verified performance in line with GPT-4o-mini and above Claude Sonnet 4.5. This should be interpreted carefully: SimpleQA is a factual precision benchmark, not a full proxy for broad capability. Still, it directly supports the paper’s claim that externalized factual memory can produce high-precision knowledge access without requiring a much larger parametric model.

Third, scaling beyond Wikipedia works. Extending pretraining from Wikipedia to FineWeb-Edu further improves factual precision. This is one of the most important results in the paper because it directly addresses the main limitation of Rel-LMLM: dependence on corpora where relational supervision is easy to synthesize.

The most useful ablation-level takeaways are mechanistic rather than cosmetic. Continuous queries reduce inference overhead relative to textual relational queries. Supervising retrieval targets without fixing the query form yields more flexible retrieval behavior. And the fact that NLU utility is largely preserved while factual precision rises suggests a better allocation of model capacity away from brute-force memorization and toward retrieval-conditioned use of knowledge. When reading the experiments, it is better to focus on the joint movement of perplexity, factual precision, and utility across scales and corpora than on any single benchmark number.1b:T697,Start with the method overview and the inference diagram. The critical sequence is: emit <FACT>, use the hidden state as a query, retrieve a textual span, splice it into context, and continue decoding. That sequence explains why the method remains compatible with standard decoder-only training and inference rather than requiring a separate retrieval stack.

Then read the data construction and objective sections carefully. The important distinction is between the LM loss, which teaches the model to use retrieved facts in context, and the contrastive retrieval loss, which teaches the hidden-state query to align with the right fact span. The real innovation is not merely adding a contrastive loss; it is removing the need to predefine the query representation during preprocessing.

After that, go to the experiments comparing Wikipedia and FineWeb-Edu and the head-to-head results against Rel-LMLM. Those sections make it clear which old bottlenecks are actually resolved: data scalability, retrieval flexibility, and factual precision. If time is limited, prioritize the main results table, the corpus-scaling experiment, and the discussion of controllability and unlearning.

There are several good follow-up questions to keep in mind. Whether top-1 retrieval becomes a ceiling in ambiguous or multi-hop settings. Whether inserting fact spans back into context creates a new context-budget bottleneck. How the construction of contrastive negatives shapes retrieval geometry. And whether the model truly reduces parametric memorization rather than simply learning to both memorize and retrieve. Any later probing or memorization analysis on this point would be especially useful.1c:T454,At each denoising step, the target distribution is the constrained mean-field posterior p_θ(x^0 | x^t, C) ∝ p_θ(x^0 | x^t) · 1[x^0 ∈ C], where the constraint C is represented by a finite automaton. The paper treats the automaton as a graphical model whose support is exactly the set of valid sequences, then performs exact inference on this chain-structured model to obtain per-token marginals under the constrained distribution. These marginals are used for both greedy and stochastic sampling.

Naive forward–backward inference on a chain requires O(L) sequential steps. Inspired by depth-reduction techniques for arithmetic circuits, the paper restructures the linear computation into a binary tree over prefix–suffix state pairs, reducing the number of inference steps to O(log L) without changing the sampling distribution. The resulting algorithm supports exact constraint satisfaction by construction, works with parallel and block-wise remasking schedules under arbitrary schedules, and exposes per-token constrained marginals that can be used as confidence scores for remasking decisions.1d:T5dd,TF-Engram is built around three requirements. First, memory construction is train-free: phrase-specific semantic entries are built offline from external corpora without optimizing the backbone model or training adapters. Second, memory fidelity is preserved by avoiding aggressive hash compression. Instead of mapping many unrelated phrases into a small GPU-resident hash table, the system keeps more precise phrase entries and moves the capacity burden to a storage hierarchy. Third, access latency is handled with a GPU–DRAM–SSD memory hierarchy and predictive prefetching.

The paper distinguishes Engram-style memory from RAG. RAG retrieves text chunks and appends them to the prompt, increasing context length and attention cost. Engram-style memory retrieves compact phrase-level signals and injects them into the model’s hidden states. This makes it better aligned with stable short-granularity knowledge such as technical terms, entity phrases, and frequent semantic patterns. It also differs from adapters or LoRA-like methods because it aims to avoid any additional training.

The system component is Early-Exit Guided Predictive Prefetching. During autoregressive decoding, early or intermediate model signals are used to predict which phrase memories are likely to be needed later, allowing entries to be fetched from DRAM or NVMe SSD before they are on the critical path. The intended effect is to scale memory beyond GPU capacity without making every token wait on external lookup.1e:T773,TriRoute 的核心是一个共享 controller。对每个 layer、每个 token，它同时输出三类决策。第一是 attention mode，候选包括 skip、local-window、full，决定该 token 在当前层使用多少注意力分辨率。第二是 FFN expert routing，在 E 个专家中选稀疏子集，并引入一个 null expert，把 MoD 式的 FFN skip 视为专家选择的特例，而不是额外分支。第三是 KV bit-width，决定该 token 自己写入 cache 时用 2/4/8/16 bit 中哪一档，从而影响未来 token 检索它时的保真度。这个设计的关键不是把三件事并排放在一起，而是共享同一份 token 表征，让路由器学到“什么 token 值得在哪些资源上花钱”。

训练上有三块技术处理。其一，三类决策的离散空间异质性很强：expert routing 是大类别稀疏选择，attention mode 和 bit-width 是小规模但代价差异很不均匀的分类。作者采用按轴定制的 straight-through / Gumbel-Softmax 松弛，并做温度退火，尽量让梯度尺度可比。其二，作者强调 cross-axis collapse cascade：如果 attention router 先学会大面积 skip，下游 FFN router 看到的输入分布会退化，进一步失去区分能力，bit router 也会变得无意义。为此论文设计了 coupling-aware balancing objective，不只做单轴 load balancing，而是抑制一个轴塌缩后拖垮其他轴。其三，预算控制采用统一可微 cost model，把 FLOPs 和 KV memory 合成单一成本，再用在线 Lagrangian dual 调节，使目标平均预算 C* 成为唯一外部旋钮。这样用户不需要分别调 expert sparsity、depth sparsity 和 quantization ratio。论文还探索 shared vs separate routers，以及 token/per-head/per-layer-group 的路由粒度，最后发现 attention 用 per-head routing，而 FFN 与 bit routing 用 token-level，是较好的折中。1f:T71b,TriRoute uses a shared controller that emits three coordinated decisions for every token at every layer. First, it chooses an attention mode: skip, local-window, or full. Second, it performs sparse FFN expert routing over E experts, with a null expert that recovers MoD-style FFN skipping as a special case. Third, it selects the bit-width used when the token writes its own key/value to the cache, choosing among 2, 4, 8, and 16 bits. The key idea is not just to place three routers side by side, but to let them share token representations so the model can learn which tokens deserve which kinds of resources.

Training this system is nontrivial because the routing spaces are heterogeneous. Expert routing is a large sparse categorical problem, while attention mode and bit-width are small categorical choices with highly uneven sensitivities. The paper uses per-axis straight-through / Gumbel-Softmax relaxations with temperature annealing to keep gradients usable. A second technical issue is cross-axis collapse. The authors argue, and empirically show, that collapse in one axis can induce collapse in the others: if attention routing becomes overly skip-heavy, the FFN router sees degraded inputs and loses discrimination, which then makes bit allocation less meaningful. To address this, they introduce a coupling-aware balancing objective rather than relying only on standard single-axis load balancing. Third, they define a unified differentiable cost model that combines FLOPs and KV memory, and enforce a target average budget with an online Lagrangian controller. This gives a single external budget knob. The paper also studies design choices such as shared versus separate routers and routing granularity, finding that per-head attention routing with token-level FFN and bit routing is a good trade-off.20:T51f,The experiments span decoder-only models from 160M to 1.3B parameters, trained at compute-optimal token budgets. The main empirical claim is Pareto improvement: at matched inference FLOPs and memory, TriRoute outperforms the best independently tuned combination of MoD, MoE, and KV quantization. The paper also emphasizes better preservation of tail-case accuracy, especially on rare or semantically important tokens, which is exactly where isolated sparsity methods often fail. Several ablations are important. Shared routing generally beats fully separate routers, indicating that the three axes do carry exploitable correlations, although interference appears at very high sparsity. Routing granularity matters as well: attention benefits from per-head decisions, while FFN and bit-width routing do not obviously need finer granularity than token level. Another key result is that the coupling-aware balancing loss improves training stability; without it, one routing axis can collapse early and drag the others down. The unified budget controller also appears to produce smoother cost-quality trade-offs than tuning three mechanisms independently. The paper’s emphasis is less on one headline benchmark number and more on demonstrating that joint routing is a better way to spend a fixed inference budget.21:T628,The core idea is a trajectory-level evaluation protocol. Each run records the full session: user messages, agent responses, tool calls, file edits, command executions, verification attempts, final answer, and final repository state. Evaluation combines formal verification, when objective checks exist, with LLM-written trajectory reviews and metric-specific scoring. The dimensions include things like instruction following, tool discipline, validation behavior, recovery from errors, and user-facing communication. The important design choice is that the written review is treated as a first-class artifact, not just a hidden judge score. That makes the benchmark useful for diagnosis.

A second key component is side-by-side review. Instead of only scoring one trajectory in isolation, the benchmark also compares two agents or two versions on the same task. This is well suited for model comparison, feature evaluation, and regression analysis, because many product regressions show up as local behavior changes before they show up in final pass rate.

On the task side, AgentLens focuses on production-like interactive coding workflows and uses a simulated user driven by another LLM conditioned on a persona and task objective. This follows the hidden-goal interaction style seen in benchmarks like τ-bench. It allows the benchmark to cover tasks without a natural binary outcome, such as writing documentation or explaining a codebase. The paper also includes reliability and bias checks, which is important because LLM-as-judge setups are otherwise easy to overtrust.22:T5b8,The method has three layers. The first is the institutional red-teaming protocol itself: treat a deployment rule as the intervention variable, hold everything else fixed, and measure the induced safety deficit. The second is a compact parameterization of consequence-allocation rules using three auditable coordinates. Concentration (kappa) captures how sharply loss is focused on one agent versus spread across the group. Identity salience captures whether the loss bearer is explicitly named as a structural target rather than selected anonymously or randomly. Incidence captures whether the burden falls regressively on less-resourced agents, progressively on more-resourced agents, or neutrally. This turns vague rule text into a structured design space. The third layer is the benchmark instantiation, IABench-CA. It uses a three-agent volunteer’s-dilemma game: agents hold private resources, decide how much to contribute toward a common threshold, and if the threshold is missed, a consequence rule determines who pays. The benchmark spans 228 contexts, 5 canonical rules, and 7 model populations, for 33,924 games. The paper also defines an Institutional Alignment Gap against a normative cooperative reference, and uses auto-labeled reasoning traces to inspect strategic explanations. The main technical strength is the causal isolation: the benchmark is built to study institutions as first-class experimental objects, not as incidental prompt wording.23:T471,The headline result is large rule-only effects. Changing only the consequence rule shifts mean fatality by 22 to 58 percentage points within every model population tested. That is the central empirical claim. A second result is that there is no universally safe default rule: the safest rule depends on context, including resource inequality, task stakes, and whether any single agent can cover the shortfall. A third result is that identity salience is a direct driver of targeting. In the one-shot anonymization experiment, targeted elimination drops from 81% to 22% when the rule stops naming a structural target. However, that protection weakens under repeated play, suggesting that anonymization suppresses explicit targeting but does not remove strategic exploitation over time. The paper also reports characteristic failure modes for different rule families, such as free-riding, sacrifice of weaker agents, or conservative collapse. For a skim-level read, the key takeaway is simple: with the same models and the same task, changing one sentence of institutional policy can flip collective safety outcomes by a very large margin.24:T485,(1) In zero-shot settings (unseen scenes, objects, robot embodiments), the action pathway shows significantly more focused attention on manipulation targets and contact regions than open-source VLA baselines, with no explicit attention supervision. (2) Removing Bottleneck Tokens after training does not fully eliminate manipulation-aligned attention, indicating the learned attention pattern is internalized into the shared backbone representation, rather than being purely a function of the bottleneck routing itself. (3) After fine-tuning on the RoboTwin simulation benchmark, the model achieves 91.4% success on clean scenes and 91.0% on randomized scenes, the top average performance among open-source VLA baselines. (4) Attention patterns are nearly identical before and after fine-tuning, indicating fine-tuning does not re-learn attention preferences, but only maps pre-formed attention patterns to executable actions. (5) The model achieves non-zero success rates on zero-shot cross-scene, cross-object, cross-embodiment tasks where most baselines fail completely, though a clear gap remains between aligned attention and reliable action execution.25:T87f,The method has three main pieces. The first is a task-unified single-stream diffusion transformer. Text conditions, image conditions, and visual latent patches are projected into the same hidden space and concatenated into one token sequence. T2I, T2V, and I2V are all handled in one framework, with image generation treated as the special case of a one-frame video. To separate condition tokens from visual tokens without introducing task-specific branches, the model uses 3D MM-RoPE with non-overlapping temporal coordinate ranges. The motivation for the single-stream design is practical and clear: better parameter reuse, denser cross-modal interaction at every layer, larger and cleaner GEMMs, and less split/concat overhead than dual-stream architectures, especially under distributed sequence-parallel training.

The second piece is sparse MoE scaling. The provided text does not include the full routing details, but the intent is clear: MoE is used to scale spatiotemporal modeling capacity without paying dense-compute cost everywhere. This mirrors the logic of MoE in LLMs, but the video setting is harder because routing has to coexist with spatial, temporal, and multimodal structure. Stability, load balancing, and temporal consistency are likely more delicate here. Choosing a single-stream backbone before adding MoE is a sensible systems decision, because it avoids compounding communication and partitioning complexity that a dual-stream design would introduce.

The third piece is data and training signal design. The model is trained on a robot-augmented corpus that mixes internet-scale videos with robot manipulation, navigation, and egocentric data. Importantly, the paper frames this as a profiling, filtering, and rebalancing problem rather than naive concatenation. On top of that, the training objective includes a multi-dimensional reward system that extends beyond perceptual quality and text alignment to physical plausibility and task-level success. Conceptually, this looks like alignment for a generative video model, but the target is still pretraining behavior: learning dynamics and interactions that are more useful for embodied settings.26:T4b0,The method has two stages. First, the authors build OpenCoF-17K, a dataset of 17,312 videos across 11 task families. Each example contains an initial conditioning image, a textual prompt, and a target reasoning video. Videos are normalized to 480p, 15 fps, and 81 frames to reduce optimization mismatch across sources. The curation pipeline combines instance-based rendering, expert-guided rendering, procedural scene synthesis, and repurposed existing videos. Wan2.2-I2V-A14B is then fine-tuned on this dataset to produce Wan-CoF, testing whether diverse temporal supervision alone can elicit Chain-of-Frame behavior. Second, the paper studies two reasoning-token designs for DiT-based video generation. Visual Reasoning Tokens are inserted into the visual latent sequence and are meant to capture lower-level visual cues. Textual Reasoning Tokens are appended to the text-conditioning sequence and provide higher-level semantic priors. The two designs intervene at different places: one directly reshapes latent visual generation, while the other modulates denoising through the conditioning path. Attention analysis is used to inspect their effects across layers, denoising steps, space, and time.27:T8d2,环境定义在符号 alphabet 上。每个 source symbol 映射到一个或多个全局唯一的多字符 RHS string；全局唯一性保证 contraction 是无歧义的，即任何匹配 RHS 的 substring 都能唯一映射回 source symbol。模型支持两类 primitive inverse operations：expansion 把一个 symbol 替换为 RHS，contraction 把一个匹配 substring 收缩回 source symbol。每一步都序列化为带位置、span 和 rule 的动作，因此生成轨迹可以逐步审计。

预训练从零开始，在 primitive rewrite chains 上训练 Transformer。数据生成器从初始 word 出发，反复采样合法 primitive expansion 或 contraction，并把 resulting word 作为下一步输入。预训练只暴露局部 grammar-consistent dynamics，不包含后续分析中的非 primitive shortcut。作者用 contraction weight ρ 控制预训练分布；默认 ρ=4，它局部提高 contraction move 的采样倾向，但不直接指定最终 contraction frequency 或 chaining length。

post-training 任务是 goal-directed contraction。prompt 由目标 symbol c* 经多次 primitive expansion 得到较长 word，模型只看到 w_prompt，需要生成逐步 contraction trace，最后输出 c*。reward 只有最终答案是否正确的二值信号。由于语法完全可审计，每条轨迹可分成 primitive rule use、valid non-primitive composition 和 invalid rewrite。valid composition 又分为 sequential composition，即把有序 primitive contraction chain 合成一个 macro step；以及 parallel composition，即在一个 emitted step 中合并彼此独立的 primitive contractions。这个 taxonomy 是全文的关键，它把“RL 学到新策略”从 pass rate 变化变成可检查的轨迹结构变化。

方法比较上，RL 与 rejection fine-tuning 形成了清晰对照。RFT 会选择成功样本，也会复制一些 shortcut-like 行为，但其中相当一部分是 spurious rewrite。RL，特别是 GRPO 的 same-prompt contrast，会在同一 prompt 的候选组内相对提高有效结构、压低无效 passenger actions。作者给出 finite-group view 来解释这种选择效应：RL 的优势不只是探索更多，而是把探索质量集中到可复用的合法组合上。28:T6cd,实验设计围绕 difficulty 和轨迹分类展开。Difficulty 由 optimal primitive contraction solution 在 256-token generation budget 下的长度定义：Difficulty 1 的 primitive 最优解能放进预算；Difficulty 2 比预算多一个 primitive contraction step；Difficulty 3 多两个 step。RL 训练 mixture 为 60% Difficulty 1、20% Difficulty 2、20% Difficulty 3。评估覆盖 held-out Difficulty 1–6，其中包含训练分布之外的更难 bucket。

关键结果有四点。第一，RL 能解决一些 pretrained model 即使用更大 sampling budget 也很少解决的 held-out problems，且收益在训练后期更明显，并随难度上升而扩大。这支持 finite-budget frontier expansion，而不只是小 k 下的重排序。第二，RFT 早期提升明显，但较快 plateau；它会保留成功轨迹中的 shortcut-like 行为，却无法稳定区分 valid composition 和 spurious rewrite。第三，轨迹分析显示 RL 有阶段性：先增强 primitive reductions，再发现和复用 valid compositions，最后把 sequential macro 与 parallel composition 固化为更稳定的策略。第四，预训练分布会影响后续策略可达性。ρ=4 的默认设置提供了更偏 contraction 的 procedural substrate；第 7 节进一步消融 ρ，检验预训练中 contraction chaining 的组织方式是否影响 RL 后续能否压缩为 macro。

提供内容中没有给出 Transformer 参数量、预训练 token 数、训练步数或具体 pass-rate 数字。因此最可靠的数字是环境和训练设置：256-token generation budget、ρ=4、RL 训练难度配比 60/20/20、评估 Difficulty 1–6，以及 pretraining 只含 primitive chains 而不含非 primitive shortcuts。29:T9d1,The environment is defined over a symbolic alphabet. Each source symbol maps to one or more globally unique multi-character right-hand-side strings. Global uniqueness makes primitive contraction unambiguous: any substring matching a right-hand side maps back to exactly one source symbol. The environment supports two inverse primitive operations. Expansion replaces a symbol with one of its right-hand sides. Contraction replaces a matching substring with its source symbol. Each step is serialized with the relevant position, span, and rule, which makes trajectory-level auditing possible.

A Transformer is pretrained from scratch on primitive rewrite chains. The generator starts from an initial word, repeatedly samples valid primitive expansions or contractions, appends the serialized action, and uses the resulting word as the next input. Pretraining exposes the model to local grammar-consistent dynamics, but not to the non-primitive shortcuts analyzed later. A contraction weight ρ controls the pretraining distribution; the default is ρ=4. This locally upweights contraction moves when both directions are available, without directly fixing the realized contraction frequency or chain length.

Post-training uses a goal-directed contraction task. A prompt is produced by expanding a target symbol c* into a longer word w_prompt. The model sees only w_prompt and must generate a contraction trace whose final emitted symbol is c*. The reward is binary and depends only on the final answer. Since the grammar is exactly known, each generated trajectory can be classified into primitive rule use, valid non-primitive composition, and invalid rewrite. Valid composition has two forms: sequential composition, which collapses an ordered chain of primitive contractions into a macro step, and parallel composition, which combines independent primitive contractions in one emitted step. This taxonomy is the technical core of the paper because it turns the vague claim of “new reasoning strategy” into an auditable trajectory property.

The comparison with rejection fine-tuning is important. RFT selects successful samples and can copy shortcut-like rewrites, but many of these are spurious. RL, especially with GRPO-style same-prompt contrast, concentrates probability mass on valid reusable structure and suppresses invalid passenger actions. The finite-group view explains why the advantage is not merely larger exploration volume, but better selection among behaviors produced for the same prompt.2a:T767,The experiments are organized around difficulty and trajectory structure. Difficulty is defined by the length of the optimal primitive contraction solution under a 256-token generation budget. Difficulty 1 problems have an optimal primitive solution that fits within the budget. Difficulty 2 requires one extra primitive contraction step beyond the budget. Difficulty 3 requires two extra steps. The RL training mixture contains 60% Difficulty 1, 20% Difficulty 2, and 20% Difficulty 3 examples. Evaluation covers held-out Difficulty 1–6, including difficulties beyond the training distribution.

The main findings are clear. First, RL solves held-out problems that the pretrained model rarely solves even with much larger sampling budgets. The gains emerge late in training and become larger as difficulty increases, which supports finite-budget frontier expansion rather than only small-k reranking. Second, RFT improves early but plateaus. It imitates successful traces, including shortcut-like behavior, but does not reliably separate valid compositions from spurious rewrites. Third, trace analysis shows a phased mechanism: RL first strengthens primitive reductions, then discovers and reuses valid compositions, and eventually consolidates sequential macros and parallel compositions into stable strategies. Fourth, the pretraining distribution matters. The default ρ=4 setting creates a contraction-biased procedural substrate, and the ablation in Section 7 tests whether the organization of contraction chaining affects which strategies RL can later compress and reuse.

The provided content does not specify model parameter count, pretraining token count, training steps, or exact pass-rate values. The reliable numerical details are the 256-token generation budget, ρ=4, the RL training mixture of 60/20/20 over Difficulties 1–3, and held-out evaluation over Difficulties 1–6.2b:T539,This paper is worth a careful read. Start with the rewrite grammar and primitive action definitions, especially global uniqueness, serialized expansion and contraction actions, and the reason these choices make every generated step auditable. Then read the post-training task setup and difficulty definition. The 256-token budget and the Difficulty 1–3 construction explain why macro composition is required rather than merely convenient.

The second key part is the trajectory taxonomy: primitive, macro, parallel, and spurious. This is where the paper provides evidence about strategy formation rather than relying only on pass@k. The third key part is the RL-versus-RFT comparison, especially why RFT plateaus and how GRPO-style same-prompt contrast suppresses spurious passenger actions. The fourth key part is the pretraining-distribution ablation in Section 7, which is the most directly relevant part for pretraining researchers.

Two follow-up directions are worth considering. One is mapping the procedural substrate in this symbolic environment to real pretraining data, such as code, mathematical derivations, tool-use traces, or verifiable synthetic trajectories. The other is using this kind of trace audit to separate genuine strategy formation from formatting gains, shortcuts, and reward hacking in natural-language RL.2c:T4c8,The paper formalizes the setup with a candidate SFT pool, an online selection policy, a fixed token budget, and the resulting fine-tuned model. It defines a behavior vector covering helpfulness, refusal rate on harmful queries, over-refusal on benign queries, verbosity, truthfulness, sycophancy, jailbreak robustness, and calibration, then defines alignment drift relative to a same-budget random selection baseline. The central claim is that online selection reweights the supervised objective and therefore encodes an implicit preference over assistant behaviors; a first-order bound links behavioral drift to attribute enrichment in the selected subset.

The empirical study compares representative families of selectors: random, loss-based, quality-based, and diversity-based. It introduces Alignment Drift Auditing (ADA), a controlled protocol that matches selectors on data budget, measures behavioral outcomes, and audits the selected data for over-represented response attributes. Building on those findings, the paper proposes Alignment-Aware Selection (AAS), which retains data-efficiency objectives while adding constraints on refusal, verbosity, truthfulness, and sycophancy to avoid one-sided behavioral drift.2d:T62b,The setup follows GKD-style multi-teacher on-policy distillation. The student samples continuations from its current policy, and teachers provide next-token distribution targets on those sampled trajectories. Each example is routed by a teacher tag to either a tool-call teacher or a response teacher. Both teachers start from the same base model and are specialized on structured tool-call targets or direct natural-language answers. A small supervised loss is also used as a format anchor for tool-call serialization.

The paper introduces the diagnosis of behavior leverage imbalance. The authors check several plausible aggregate explanations: whether tool-call samples receive more token exposure, whether full-sequence per-token JSD is larger for the tool teacher, and whether a gradient proxy is larger. These explanations do not match the observed behavior; in some runs, response tokens receive more exposure or larger aggregate divergence. The proposed explanation is that local signals near the decision boundary have higher behavioral leverage than average sequence-level signals.

The mitigation is Soft Clamp. For token divergence d_i, the method defines a batch-level threshold C = k * mean(d). Divergences below C are left unchanged. Divergences above C are transformed as d'_i = d_i * C / stopgrad(d_i). The forward contribution is capped around C, while the backward gradient is scaled down rather than removed. This differs from hard clipping, which can erase difficult-token gradients, and from global reweighting, which does not target extreme local signals.2e:T450,GeoSD combines two terms defined in a shared geometry over next-token distributions:
1) Local control via Hellinger distillation: gradients are implicitly weighted by teacher–student probability overlap. Tokens that the teacher assigns high probability to but the student assigns near-zero probability no longer dominate the update; shared mass transfers strongly and grows as agreement increases.
2) Global control via a proximal penalty: a Fisher–Rao distance to a recent checkpoint constrains accumulated movement (drift) in predictive distributions over training.
3) Optimization alignment: since both terms live in the same geometry, the paper motivates natural-gradient-style updates to make each step the smallest necessary change in prediction space while improving the objective.
Mechanistically, the paper argues KL matching often achieves agreement by collapsing alternatives (draining probability mass), producing brittle, overconfident policies; GeoSD strengthens the teacher’s top choice while keeping plausible alternatives “within reach,” which matters under distribution shift.2f:T945,方法名是 AdaPrefix-GRPO。核心设计有四步。第一，作者先分析 GRPO 信号与组内成功率 k/G 的关系。采用未做 std-normalization 的 centered advantage 时，正向学习信号随 p(1-p) 变化，在 p≈0.5 附近最大；这也是他们把目标 solve rate 设在约 50% 的原因。这里有一个细节：如果用按组标准差归一化的 advantage，极低成功率时单个正确样本的 advantage 会被放大，数值上更不稳定，而未归一化形式更适合把“中等成功率最有信号”这个结论直接落到控制目标上。

第二，作者把参考解前缀注入 prompt，作为 assistant message 的部分答案，让模型只补全后半段；前缀 token 不参与梯度，reward 仍按完整输出的最终答案计算。这样做的好处是实现层面几乎不改 trainer，只改数据管线和 loss mask。与直接做 trace SFT 不同，它没有把整条参考解当监督目标压给模型，而是只在条件分布上降低问题难度，保留了 RL 的探索空间。

第三，作者不是给每题固定一个 prefix ratio，而是做“全局基准前缀 + 每题静态偏移”的两级控制。训练前先做一次 cold-start calibration，根据 base model 在不同题上的条件成功率，给每个样本估一个 difficulty-dependent offset；训练时再维护一个全局 base prefix length，用 secant 或 binary search 这类 root-finding 更新规则，根据最近 batch 实际观测到的平均 k/G，把全局前缀往目标 solve rate 推。这样既避免逐题在线搜索的高成本，也比完全固定的 per-sample 前缀更能跟踪策略随时间的变化。

第四，训练后期把所有前缀 anneal 到 0，保证部署时模型不依赖提示拐杖。这个 annealing 很关键。如果一直带前缀训练，模型学到的是“条件补全参考解”的能力，而不是无辅助求解；逐步撤掉前缀，相当于把课程从 teacher-assisted completion 过渡到独立解题。

和并行工作相比，差别主要在控制论视角。已有 prefix-based RL 方法通常在训练开始前选几个固定前缀长度，要求 base accuracy 落在“还行”的范围；AdaPrefix 的主张是，非零信号不够，应该把样本尽量维持在接近 50% 成功率的高梯度区，而且这个点会随训练漂移，所以必须在线调节。30:T8b1,The method, AdaPrefix-GRPO, has four key pieces. First, the paper analyzes how GRPO signal depends on the within-group success rate k/G. With unnormalized centered advantages, the aggregate positive learning mass scales like p(1-p), peaking near p=0.5. That is why the controller targets roughly a 50% solve rate. The choice to avoid std-normalized advantages matters here: normalization can make rare successes numerically dominant when p is tiny, while the unnormalized form preserves a cleaner and more stable signal-shape argument.

Second, the method prepends a correct prefix from a reference solution as an assistant message and asks the model to complete the rest. Gradients are masked on the prefix tokens, while reward is still computed from the final full answer. This is an important design choice. It lowers conditional difficulty without turning the whole trace into a supervised target, so it preserves more of the exploration behavior that plain trace SFT tends to suppress.

Third, the controller is not fully per-sample online and not fully static either. The paper uses a two-level scheme: a global base prefix length plus a fixed per-sample offset derived from a one-time calibration. During training, the system observes the realized batch mean k/G and updates the global base prefix using a root-finding style rule such as secant or binary search, pushing the observed solve rate toward the target. The per-sample offsets account for persistent difficulty differences, while the global term tracks the non-stationarity induced by policy improvement.

Fourth, all prefixes are annealed to zero by the end of training, so the deployed model solves problems unaided. This is crucial. Without annealing, the policy may become good at conditioned continuation of partially solved traces rather than independent problem solving.

Relative to concurrent prefix-based RL work, the distinction is the control perspective. Prior methods typically choose a few fixed prefix lengths so that the base model has a “reasonable” conditioned accuracy. AdaPrefix argues that merely making the signal non-zero is not enough; one should keep examples near the high-signal regime, and that regime drifts during training.31:T77f,The experiments focus on hard math and are explicitly run under matched training FLOPs, which is important because otherwise gains could be explained by extra sampling or longer training. Baselines include vanilla GRPO and a fixed-prefix variant. The headline result is that AdaPrefix-GRPO more than doubles pass@1 over vanilla GRPO on held-out hard problems, while also beating the fixed-prefix baseline. At the same time, pass@16 does not collapse, suggesting the method is not simply trading diversity for a narrow pass@1 gain. The paper also reports that the gains grow as model size shrinks, which matches the mechanism: smaller models have a larger dead zone, so converting impossible examples into learnable ones yields larger marginal benefit.

Several ablations are especially informative. Targeting an intermediate solve rate works better than aiming too low or too high, supporting the p(1-p) analysis. Fixed prefixes help early but drift toward the too-easy regime as the policy improves, reducing learning signal; the adaptive controller keeps the observed batch solve rate near the target. Annealing prefixes to zero is necessary for strong unaided test-time performance. The one-time calibration cost is treated as model-level preprocessing rather than run-specific training cost, since it can be reused across runs on the same model and dataset.

A useful quantitative anchor from the paper is that vanilla GRPO solves only about 35% of problems at pass@16 on the hard split, indicating a substantial dead zone in the training distribution. AdaPrefix’s benefit comes from moving a meaningful fraction of that region into a non-degenerate regime where group-relative learning signal exists. The excerpt does not expose the full parameter table or every absolute number, but the evaluation setup appears careful about compute matching, model-size stratification, and the no-prefix deployment constraint.32:T4dc,Start with the Introduction and Section 4.1. Those parts make the central argument clearly: why GRPO fails on hard examples, and why the target is not just “non-zero success” but “success near 50%.” Then read Section 4.2 for the actual recipe: cold-start calibration, global closed-loop prefix adaptation, and annealing to zero.

For figures, prioritize the plots showing how solve rate changes with prefix length and the training curves showing batch-mean k/G being regulated near the target. Those are the core validity checks. Then inspect the main results table to verify that comparisons are truly under matched FLOPs and that both pass@1 and pass@16 are reported. In the ablations, focus on target solve rate, fixed versus adaptive prefixing, annealing versus no annealing, and how gains vary with model size.

Estimated reading time is 20–30 minutes. Two deeper questions are worth keeping in mind. First, does prefixing introduce a style or solution-path bias from the reference trace, especially on problems with many valid derivations? Second, can the same control framework be generalized to code, tool use, or even pretraining settings where one can regulate corruption strength, mask ratio, or other difficulty variables?33:T7c5,Agon trains two policies head-to-head with competitive GRPO. On each problem, one policy acts as a drafter and produces a worked solution; the other acts as a challenger, reads the opponent’s draft, and then attempts the same problem. Reward depends not only on correctness but also on out-solving the rival. Roles alternate across training steps so both policies learn both drafting and challenging.

The paper argues that two ingredients are necessary. First, there must be information exchange: the challenger needs access to the opponent’s reasoning, otherwise the setup cannot say anything about whether the trace itself is useful or exploitable. Second, the interaction must be competitive rather than cooperative. Pure cooperation or agreement-seeking tends to regress toward consensus and can dilute quality when the two models differ in strength. Competitive pressure instead rewards reasoning that remains strong even when exposed to a peer.

To isolate these effects, the paper uses a 2×2 design over competition versus cooperation and exchange versus no exchange. One cell, competition without exchange, is argued to be inert under group-relative normalization, so the meaningful comparison is adversarial exchange versus cooperative exchange. That analytical point matters because it prevents over-interpreting a control that is structurally unable to carry the intended signal.

Implementation is deliberately lightweight. Rather than training two full models, the authors instantiate the pair as two LoRA-style adapters on top of one frozen base, with roughly 2% extra memory overhead instead of 2× model cost. Different initialization and separate update streams are used to keep the two policies divergent. The communication channel is plain text in this version; latent-space exchange is mentioned as a future direction. Importantly, the optimization core remains a stock GRPO trainer. The novelty is in rollout structure and reward shaping, not in a new optimizer.34:T661,The experiments are run on hard math under matched generation budgets. The main findings are threefold. First, dual-model training with information exchange outperforms single-model vanilla GRPO. Second, given exchange, competitive training outperforms cooperative training, supporting the claim that adversarial pressure is more effective than mutual assistance for improving reasoning. Third, the gains are larger for smaller models, which is plausible because smaller models have lower reasoning ceilings and benefit more from an external source of signal.

A particularly useful result from the abstract is that, at the same doubled inference budget, the trained draft-and-challenge cascade nearly doubles the accuracy of an untrained Mixture-of-Agents orchestration over the same base, while the untrained orchestration itself barely improves over GRPO. That suggests the gain is not merely from adding another pass or another model at inference time; it comes from the co-training dynamics and the induced specialization under competition.

The evaluation design includes self-refinement as a control, which is important for the paper’s argument that self-auditing tends to plateau. It also includes zero-training orchestration and a budget-matched self-cascade GRPO control, helping separate “more inference structure” from “competitive joint training.” The excerpt does not expose the full parameter scales, training lengths, or all absolute scores, but it is clear that the paper tracks pass@1, reasoning length, and fairness under matched generation budgets rather than relying on a single cherry-picked endpoint.35:T5d5,Start with the Introduction, especially the framing of the “unscored trace” problem. That section explains clearly why outcome-only reward can favor longer traces without improving reasoning quality. Then read the method section with three keywords in mind: draft-and-challenge, role rotation, and competitive reward. After that, go straight to the 2×2 experiment design to understand why competition and exchange must be disentangled, and why one control cell is structurally inert.

For figures and tables, prioritize the main results and any plots relating trace length to accuracy. If the paper reports token-normalized gains or changes in length distribution, those are more informative than pass@1 alone because they speak directly to the claimed mechanism. Then inspect the controls: self-refinement, cooperative exchange, and untrained orchestration. Those comparisons determine whether the method is learning better reasoning or merely exploiting extra context and extra passes.

Estimated reading time is 20–30 minutes. Three deeper questions are worth probing. First, do the two adapters remain meaningfully different over training, or do they gradually collapse toward similar policies? Second, can the competitive reward induce adversarially misleading traces that help beat the rival without improving true task-general reasoning? Third, since the challenger reads the draft in text, how much of the gain comes from extra conditional information versus competition itself?36:T46c,Pyligent implements a validated-search view of reasoning inspired by the Diligent Learner formulation. It maintains a ChainTree of partial reasoning states. The model emits one action at a time: <node> appends an intermediate step, <done> proposes a final answer, and <backtrack> returns control to an earlier node. Training has three stages. SFT-A teaches the action format and gives the model a nonzero probability of producing valid next steps. Validator-guided exploration samples continuations from selected prefixes and labels both successful continuations and failed leaves. SFT-B then trains on examples extracted from the resulting tree: success pairs, failure pairs with validator-derived backtrack targets, and optional continuation-after-trace pairs. A reverse curriculum starts exploration near the end of a gold chain and gradually moves toward the root. At inference time, the runtime validates backtrack targets and truncates the context accordingly. Traced recovery adds a compact trace after truncation, summarizing the abandoned branch and validator reason, so the model is less likely to repeat the same mistake.37:T563,Each RoPE frequency is modeled as a positional lens with an inherent field-resolution tradeoff: higher frequencies offer sharper local position discrimination but have shorter effective range due to fast phase wrapping, while lower frequencies cover longer ranges but have coarser resolution for nearby positions. The work defines the positional dependency profile of training data as the width W of the distribution of task-relevant dependencies across relative positions. Analysis of positional contrast in attention scores proves a frequency matching principle: for a dependency profile of width W, the optimal selected frequency scales as θ* ∝ 1/W, meaning training automatically selects frequency bands matched to the dependency scales present in the data. The analysis of PI shows the operation scales each frequency's effective field by a factor of α while reducing resolution by the same factor. PI enables valid length generalization only if test-time dependency profiles are an α-stretched (self-similar) version of training-time profiles, which maps training-optimal frequencies to test-optimal frequencies. The work also introduces an attention score-level energy metric to quantify frequency usage, controlling for initialization effects and input-specific variation to show frequency usage patterns are stable after convergence and determined by training data.38:T5b5,This is a measurement-validity audit rather than a new metric or training recipe. The authors separate two claims and test them with long-run trajectories:
1) Measurement hazard (overstatement): embedding effective rank at T_grok can be far above its eventual plateau. In modular arithmetic sweeps, at-grok ranks are uniformly high and poorly separated across conditions, while long training drives many fully-generalizing runs down to a low-rank floor; boundary runs that never compress stay high-rank. This makes at-grok readings overstate converged complexity by ~3–5× and can erase the key “compress vs. not” distinction.
2) Timing result (lag): they directly challenge a specific interpretation that compression coincides with the accuracy transition. With higher temporal resolution and longer training, they find rank compression lags the accuracy jump by large margins (≥1e4 steps, on the order of T_grok).
They also guard against a self-referential pitfall: the ‘floor’ can shift. Some low-budget MLP settings show a second late-stage collapse (with global weight norm roughly doubling), implying that an audit must verify a genuine plateau rather than picking an arbitrary late window.
A positive deliverable is an analysis of what controls lag size under a harness that varies one architectural factor at a time, using quantities like the fraction of compression completed by the grok step (introduced around Sec.6 in the provided Communicating gradients in low-precision formats, such as FP8 and NVFP4, can significantly reduce the communication volume. Existing methods quantize gradients via linear or nonlinear mappings in Euclidean space, often degrading model performance because highly anisotropic gradients incur direction-dependent distortion. We present GIFT, a geometry-informed gradient scaling method that performs low-p","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2607_07494.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"分布式 LLM 预训练中，梯度通信占整体训练时间的比例可达 40%（128 卡 A100 训练 8.3B 模型场景）。使用 FP8 等低精度格式压缩梯度是直接的带宽优化手段，但现有方法在欧氏空间中做线性/非线性量化，会因梯度分布的高度各向异性（不同方向的方差差异大）引入方向相关的失真，导致同步后的更新方向偏离高精度基准，最终损害下游任务性能。同时，多数已有工作同时量化权重、激活、优化器状态，难以单独评估低精度梯度本身的影响，也难以平衡额外计算开销与通信收益。","importance":"这是首个从黎曼几何视角系统诊断低精度梯度量化误差来源的工作，明确指出欧氏空间均匀缩放与各向异性梯度几何之间的不匹配是性能下降的核心原因。方法层面提供了一种不修改优化器、模型结构、训练配方、通信原语和数值格式的纯坐标变换方案，可直接对接 NCCL 2.24 原生 FP8 通信，工程侵入性极低。实践层面给出了“选择性几何变换”的成本-收益折中思路，让 K-FAC 类近似的额外计算开销可被通信收益覆盖，为大规模预训练中 FP8 梯度通信的落地提供了可复现的路径。","technical_approach":"核心思路是在量化前将梯度变换到近似各向同性的坐标系，使 FP8 均匀缩放对不同维度引入的误差量级相当。具体实现分三步：1）误差诊断：通过 Fisher 信息矩阵白化将梯度映射到黎曼流形，验证该空间下梯度分布接近各向同性，量化误差显著低于欧氏空间；2）几何近似：采用 K-FAC 对 Fisher 矩阵做 Kronecker 分解近似，仅用于构造临时通信坐标系，不替换优化器的更新规则（仍使用 Muon 优化器的原生更新）；3）选择性应用：先逐层评估量化敏感度，仅对量化误差敏感的层执行 K-FAC 变换与逆变换，其余层沿用标准逐层欧氏缩放，控制额外计算量。整个流程只改变梯度通信时的坐标表示，不影响优化器状态存储和模型权重精度。","experiments":"基于 Megatron-LM 实现，在 OpenWebText 数据集上预训练 Llama-300M 和 Llama-600M，优化器采用 Muon。误差分析显示，与欧氏逐层缩放的 FP8 基线相比，GIFT 将 FP8 梯度与 FP32 梯度的平均误差降低最多 67.4%。通信体积方面，相对 FP32 梯度减少 75%（对应 FP8 的理论压缩比）。端到端训练速度上，64 张 GH200 超芯片场景下，整体预训练时间较 FP32 减少 7.6%；同规模下直接使用欧氏 FP8 可减少 10.79% 的时间，GIFT 以约 3 个百分点的原始加速比为代价，换来了更好的下游任务性能保留。下游评估中，相同优化器和通信路径下，GIFT 预训练的模型在多数任务上的表现优于欧氏 FP8 基线，更接近 BF16/FP32 基准。","reading_guide":"重点阅读第 II 节的误差诊断部分，尤其是梯度各向异性与欧氏量化误差的对应关系推导，以及 Fisher 白化后分布变化的可视化；其次关注选择性 K-FAC 变换的敏感度评估标准和层选择策略，这是控制计算开销的关键；实验部分重点对比 GIFT、欧氏 FP8、FP32 三者的梯度误差-训练时间-下游性能三维权衡，注意区分“通信体积减少”和“端到端加速比”的差异。值得深挖的点包括：该几何变换思路是否可扩展到 NVFP4 等更低精度格式、是否可与 ZeRO 等分片数据并行策略结合、量化敏感度评估是否可在训练前离线完成以进一步降低开销。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In distributed LLM pretraining, gradient communication can account for up to 40% of total training time (as observed in a 128-A100 setup for an 8.3B GPT-style model). Compressing gradients into low-precision formats such as FP8 directly cuts bandwidth usage, but existing methods perform linear or nonlinear quantization in Euclidean space. Because gradient distributions are highly anisotropic—variances differ drastically across directions—uniform scaling introduces direction-dependent distortion that drifts the synchronized update away from the high-precision reference, degrading downstream performance. Most prior works simultaneously quantize weights, activations, and optimizer states, making it hard to isolate the impact of low-precision gradients alone, or to balance extra compute overhead against communication savings.","importance":"This work provides the first systematic diagnosis of low-precision gradient quantization error from a Riemannian geometry perspective, identifying the mismatch between anisotropic local gradient geometry and axis-aligned Euclidean scaling as the root cause of performance degradation. The proposed solution is a pure coordinate transformation that leaves the optimizer, model architecture, training recipe, communication collectives, and numerical format untouched, enabling direct integration with NCCL 2.24 native FP8 communication with minimal engineering overhead. The selective geometry-transform strategy further makes K-FAC-style approximation practical, such that the extra compute cost is offset by communication gains. It offers a reproducible path for deploying FP8 gradient communication in large-scale pretraining.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"Implemented in Megatron-LM. Llama-300M and Llama-600M models are pretrained on OpenWebText with the Muon optimizer. Error analysis shows GIFT reduces the average difference between FP8 and FP32 gradients by up to 67.4% compared with a Euclidean layer-wise scaling FP8 baseline. Gradient payload size is reduced by 75% relative to FP32, matching the theoretical FP8 compression ratio. End-to-end, on 64 GH200 superchips, GIFT cuts overall pretraining time by 7.6% over FP32; direct Euclidean FP8 achieves a 10.79% reduction at the same scale, meaning GIFT trades roughly 3 percentage points of raw speedup for better downstream preservation. Under identical optimizer and communication paths, models pretrained with GIFT outperform the Euclidean FP8 baseline on most downstream tasks and stay closer to the BF16/FP32 reference.","reading_guide":"Focus first on Section II's error diagnosis, especially the derivation linking gradient anisotropy to Euclidean quantization error and the visualizations of distribution change after Fisher whitening. Next, study the quantization-sensitivity metric and layer-selection policy for selective K-FAC application—this is the key mechanism that controls compute overhead. In experiments, examine the three-way tradeoff among gradient error, training wall time, and downstream performance across GIFT, Euclidean FP8, and FP32; pay attention to the distinction between communication-volume reduction and end-to-end speedup. Worth investigating further are: whether the geometry-transform framework extends to lower-precision formats like NVFP4, whether it composes with sharded data parallelism such as ZeRO, and whether quantization-sensitivity evaluation can be done offline before training to further reduce runtime overhead.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看方法和实验规模；若大模型结果扎实，GIFT 是低精度梯度通信里少见的低侵入改动。","en":"Read the method and scale details first; if the large-model results hold, GIFT is a rare low-intrusion fix for low-precision gradient communication."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Jieying Wang","Shuyuan Fan","Mingkai Zheng","Zhao Zhang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["llm-pretraining","gradient-communication","low-precision","distributed-training","quantization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07494","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.07494","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}}]},{"id":"scaling","emoji":"📈","name":"Scaling 与计算分配","description":"Scaling Law / compute-optimality（Chinchilla/Kaplan/µP）、数据与模型规模扩展、test-time/inference-time scaling、agent/RL scaling、scaling 系数与可预测性方法论","papers":[{"id":"2607.06720","title":"When Does In-Context Search Help? ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_07707.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是：如何把“知识外置”的 LMLM 范式从依赖关系型 KB、依赖 Wikipedia 实体结构、依赖预先写死查询路径的设定，推进到更可扩展、更通用的预训练框架。Rel-LMLM 的关键问题有三层。第一，知识表示受限，只能外置成 subject-relation-object 一类关系片段，很多自由文本中的事实无法自然落到这种结构里。第二，检索表达受限，模型必须生成离散的自然语言查询，既增加推理时 token 开销，也把查询空间限制在人工定义的关系模板内。第三，训练监督过强地规定了“该怎么查”，预处理阶段直接合成查询文本，导致模型学到的是固定检索路径，而不是更灵活的查询表示。Co-LMLM 的目标就是保留 LMLM 的可编辑、可归因、可直接 unlearning 的优点，同时把查询改成连续向量，把 KB 改成 dense key + text value 更接近一种新的基座建模范式。第二，它把知识外置从结构化关系 KB 推向自由文本事实 span，使这条路线不再被 Wikipedia 式实体页面结构卡住，具备向 FineWeb-Edu 这类大规模网页语料扩展的可能。第三，它给出了一个很少见但很重要的结果：在中小模型尺度上，外置知识不只是带来可控性，还能同时改善 perplexity 和 factual precision，而且不明显伤害 NLU Existing systems are designed for autoregressive models and assume left-to-right generation, masking out invalid next tokens at each step. Diffusion language models, however, break this assumption: they sample multiple positions simultaneously from a fully-factorized mean-field distribution at each denoising step. In this paper, we present a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_07026.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"离散扩散语言模型在每个去噪步同时从 fully-factorized mean-field 分布中采样多个位置，这使得为自回归模型设计的逐 token 约束解码失效：局部合法的 token 组合起来可能违反全局约束（如 JSON schema、SQL 语法、函数调用格式）。论文要解决的是：在 dLLM 中如何对任意可由有限自动机表达的约束，进行精确、可处理、支持贪心和随机采样的受约束后验采样。","importance":"这是 diffusion LM 从“能生成”走向“能可靠服务”的关键基础设施工作。AR 模型之所以能在工具调用、代码、结构化输出场景落地，很大程度上依赖成熟的 constrained decoding；dLLM（LLaDA、Dream、以及 Gemini Diffusion/Seed Diffusion 一类系统）如果不能做严格的结构化约束，实际部署会卡在格式错误率上。论文给出的是一个精确算法，不是启发式修补，而且在 O(log L) 并行步数内完成，意味着 constrained decoding 不会成为 dLLM 推理的主要瓶颈。这对非 AR 建模范式的工程可行性是一个明显推进。","technical_approach":"核心对象是每一步去噪时的受约束 mean-field 后验：p_θ(x^0 | x^t, C) ∝ p_θ(x^0 | x^t) · 1[x^0 ∈ C]。约束 C 由有限自动机表示，论文把自动机看成一个编码约束支撑集的图模型，在这个链状图模型上做精确推断，从而得到每个位置在受约束分布下的边缘概率，再据此进行采样。\n\n直接在链式结构上做前向–后向推断是 O(L) 串行步骤。论文借鉴算术电路的 depth reduction 思想，把这个线性计算图重构成由前缀–后缀状态对组成的二叉树，使推断步数降到 O(log L)，同时保持采样分布不变。该算法天然支持：（1）精确约束满足，因为采样严格限制在自动机接受的序列集合内；（2）贪心和随机采样两种模式；（3）并行以及 block-wise remasking 调度；（4）输出每个 token 在受约束分布下的 marginal，可作为 remasking 的置信度信号。","experiments":"实验在 LLaDA-8B 和 Dream-7B 上进行，覆盖四类约束场景：JSON schema 函数调用（xLAM、BFCL）、规划类格式约束（Sudoku、Countdown）、SQL 语法与 schema 约束（Spider）、数学符号表达式约束（GSM-Symbolic）。结果包括两部分。一是约束满足率：方法按构造保证输出满足自动机表达的结构约束。二是任务准确率提升：在 BFCL-Live 上，Dream 的 greedy 准确率从 63.9% 提升到 71.5%；随机采样下未约束 Dream 掉到 22.3%，受约束版本保持 69.0%，同时 wall-clock 开销低于 中必然失效。第二层重点看把有限自动机视为图模型后，如何在其上表示受约束后验并做 marginal 计算；这是算法正确性的来源。第三层看 O(L) 到 O(log L) 的 tree reduction，以及 per-token marginal 如何接入 remasking 策略。图表上建议重点对照 remasking 策略与 marginal 置信度配合的实验，以及随机采样下未约束模型崩塌而受约束模型稳健的数字，这最能体现方法对 dLLM 实际可用性的影响。值得注意的边界是：论文处理的是有限自动机可表达的约束；更复杂的上下文无关/语义级约束如何嵌入，是后续问题。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Discrete diffusion language models denoise multiple positions in parallel from a fully factorized mean-field distribution at each step. Standard autoregressive constrained decoding masks invalid next tokens left-to-right, which is insufficient for dLLMs: tokens that are locally valid can combine into globally invalid sequences under constraints such as JSON schemas, SQL grammars, or function-call formats. The paper addresses how to perform exact, tractable constrained posterior sampling in dLLMs for any constraint expressible as a finite automaton.","importance":"This is an enabling infrastructure result for non-autoregressive diffusion LMs. Autoregressive LLMs became deployable for tool use, code, and structured output in no small part because constrained decoding became reliable and cheap. For dLLMs such as LLaDA, Dream, and the diffusion-based systems behind Gemini Diffusion or Seed Diffusion, lack of exact structured decoding is a practical bottleneck: even strong generation quality is undermined by format violations. The paper provides an exact algorithm rather than a heuristic patch, and it does so with O(log L) parallel inference steps, so constrained decoding does not become the dominant inference cost. That materially improves the engineering viability of dLLMs as an alternative modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$1c","experiments":"Evaluation is conducted on LLaDA-8B and Dream-7B across four constraint families: JSON-schema function calling (xLAM, BFCL), planning-format constraints (Sudoku, Countdown), SQL syntax and schema constraints (Spider), and symbolic expression constraints for math (GSM-Symbolic). The method guarantees satisfaction of automaton-expressible constraints by construction. It also improves task accuracy substantially: on BFCL-Live, Dream’s greedy accuracy rises from 63.9% to 71.5%; under stochastic sampling, unconstrained Dream collapses to 22.3%, while the constrained method maintains 69.0% with less than 5% wall-clock overhead. These results indicate that constrained decoding does more than fix formatting—it removes invalid search trajectories and therefore improves effective task accuracy, especially under sampling.","reading_guide":"Read in three layers. Third, the tree reduction from O(L) to O(log L) and the use of constrained marginals as remasking signals. Pay particular attention to results under stochastic sampling, where unconstrained dLLMs degrade sharply while constrained decoding remains stable. Original GDN performs dense key-value outer product updates to the state matrix with O(dqk×dv) complexity, so increasing state size directly increases FLOPs. SDM adopts the Product-Key Memory indexing scheme, splitting the key space into two sub-codebooks to generate a large set of memory slots via outer sum, then only performs read/write operations on top-k selected slots to avoid dense matrix compute. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2607_07388.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要解决的是 LLM 静态知识扩展的成本问题：如果事实知识、领域术语和常见短语模式都隐式存进 Transformer 参数，那么更新知识通常需要继续预训练、微调、RAG 或拉长上下文。TF-Engram 试图用无需训练的短语级外部记忆，把部分静态知识从模型参数中移出来，同时避免哈希压缩带来的语义混叠和 SSD state。若系统成本可控，这类方法可以降低为补充静态知识而继续预训练的需求，也为“参数记忆、上下文记忆、外部状态记忆”之间的分工提供了一个工程化参照。它更接近 memory-augmented LM 和 serving-system 交界，而不是传统 RAG。","technical_approach":"TF-Engram 有三个设计目标。第一，train-free memory construction：从外部语料离线构建短语特定的语义记忆，不额外训练 backbone，也尽量避免绑定到某个微调过程。第二，precise phrase memory：不把大量短语压进小型 GPU 哈希表，而是保留更明确的 phrase entry，减少 unrelated phrases 落到同一 slot 后形成混合表示的问题。第三，hierarchical storage with prefetch：把大规模 memory table 放在 GPU、主机 DRAM 和 NVMe SSD 的层级存储中，用系统调度而不是单纯压缩来解决容量问题。\n\n论文强调 Engram-style memory 与 RAG 的差异。RAG 检索的是文本块，并把文本追加到 prompt，增加上下文长度和 attention 成本；Engram 则通过侧路把检索到的短语记忆注入 hidden state，更适合稳定、短粒度的 phrase-level knowledge。它也不同于 adapter 或 LoRA 这类参数高效方法，因为目标是不训练额外模块。\n\n为了解决外部存储访问延迟，TF-Engram 使用 Early-Exit Guided Predictive Prefetching。直观做法是在自回归解码早期，根据浅层或中间层信号预测后续可能需要的短语记忆，并提前从 DRAM/SSD 拉取到更近的缓存层。这样把随机外存访问从 token 关键路径中移出去，减少对逐 token 生成延迟的影响。","experiments":"提供内容显示实验使用 Qwen3-0.6B 作为 backbone，并评估 TF-Engram 在外部短语记忆注入、存储层级和预取机制下的效果。摘要和正文片段没有给出完整任务列表、准确率/困惑度提升、延迟、SSD 命中率或内存占用数字，因此不能可靠复述具体数值。\n\n从论文目标看，关键实验应关注三类指标：一是知识或领域短语相关任务上的生成质量；二是与哈希式 Engram、RAG、长上下文或无记忆 baseline 的比较；三是系统指标，包括 GPU/DRAM/SSD 命中率、prefetch 准确率、每 token Prefetching，这是论文最需要验证的系统部分。实验部分建议重点看质量-延迟-容量三者的权衡：如果质量提升依赖高访问频率，但预取命中率不足，实际部署收益会受限。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the cost of expanding static knowledge in LLMs. The authors argue that these decisions are semantically coupled. A rare entity token may not need a heavy FFN transformation, yet it may still require full attention and high-precision KV storage because future tokens must retrieve it accurately. Function words are different on all three axes. We show that a biased judge does not merely add noise; it \\emph{silently switches off the curator}. We make this precise with a corrupted","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_07436.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"研究对象是自进化 Agent 中的技能退役机制在 LLM judge 有偏时如何失效。关键点不是 judge 有噪声，而是 false-pass bias：真实失败被判为通过。对于依赖失败样本来合成新技能、统计旧技能贡献的系统，这类偏差会让 curator 看不到该退役的证据。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值有限，但对 post-train 与 agent 自改进闭环有启发。它提醒一个常被忽略的问题：如果训练或自进化信号来自 LLM judge，误差结构比平均准确率更重要。对 failure-driven 的数据合成、RL 反馈收集、自动课程构建来说，假阳性比对称噪声更危险，因为它会系统性删除负反馈。","technical_approach":"方法上把 reward corruption 拆成两类：对称翻转噪声 ρ，以及失败被误报为通过的 false-pass rate ρ_F→P。作者在 Ratchet 类技能库治理框架上分析这两种通道对 per-skill contribution estimator 的影响，并给出阈值条件：在其设定下，当 ρ_F→P 超过 (1-τ)/2，退役信号会越过不可恢复的边界。实验不是直接使用真实 LLM judge，而是在确定性 grader 上注入可控腐蚀，用来隔离 causal channel。这样能避免把模型能力、任务难度和 judge 偏差混在一起。","experiments":"主实验使用 reference-free 的长报告 section composition。数据来自 5 个完整 deep-research production，切成 155 个 evidence slices；每个 slice 固定证据、指标和允许引用。确定性 grader 检查 5 类 QC violation：orphan citation、unregistered metric、bare number、broken cross-reference、missing TL;DR。PASS 定义为零违规。由于温度 0.7 下大部分样本较易通过，作者构造 hard subset：155 个任务各采样 3 次，保留至少失败一次的 71 个任务，按报告分层成 43 train / 28 eval。结果显示，对称噪声下退役机制仍可工作；false-pass bias 会在超过阈值后关闭 curator。下游性能损害是否显性，取决于同一个腐蚀通道是否也让 synthesis 缺少失败样本。作者还用 code-generation cross-check 检查这种机制是否只存在于报告任务。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对 failure-driven loop 的拆解、Sec. 4 的 corrupted-reward 分析、Sec. 5 的 deployment go/no-go audit，以及实验中区分 contribution-retirement 和 cap-eviction churn 的部分。若关心用 LLM judge 做自训练或自动数据筛选，可以重点看 false-pass rate 的估计方法，而不是任务本身。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a failure mode in self-evolving agents: skill retirement stops working when the judge has asymmetric false-pass bias. The issue is not generic reward noise. It is the specific case where true failures are reported as passes, removing the evidence that the curator needs to retire harmful or stale skills.","importance":"The direct connection to foundation-model pretraining is limited, but the work is useful for post-training and self-improvement pipelines. It gives a concrete warning: when training signals come from LLM judges, the structure of judge error matters more than the average judge accuracy. In failure-driven data generation, RL feedback collection, or automatic curriculum construction, false positives can be more damaging than symmetric noise because they erase negative feedback at the source.","technical_approach":"The analysis separates two reward corruptions: symmetric label-flip noise ρ and false-pass bias ρ_F→P. In a Ratchet-style governed skill library, the authors analyze how these channels affect the per-skill contribution estimator. Under their channel model, once ρ_F→P exceeds (1-τ)/2, retirement cannot be recovered by more samples. The empirical design injects controlled corruption on top of a deterministic grader, rather than relying directly on a noisy LLM judge. This isolates the causal role of reward asymmetry from model capability and task difficulty.","experiments":"The main testbed is reference-free long-form report section composition. The data comes from five deep-research productions, yielding 155 frozen evidence slices. Each trial uses fixed cards, metric values, and allowed cross-references. A deterministic grader checks five QC violations: orphan citation, unregistered metric, bare number, broken cross-reference, and missing TL;DR. A pass means zero violations. Since many tasks are easy for a competent composer at temperature 0.7, the authors build a hard subset by probing all 155 tasks three times and retaining 71 tasks with at least one failure, split into 43 train and 28 evaluation tasks. Symmetric noise leaves retirement mostly intact. False-pass bias disables the curator beyond the predicted threshold. Downstream performance harm appears only when the same corruption also starves the synthesizer of observed failures. A code-generation cross-check is used to test whether the mechanism transfers outside report writing.","reading_guide":"A skim is sufficient, around 20–30 minutes. systems once human-written benchmarks saturate and human examiners can no longer reliably author tasks that are both difficult and verifiable. The paper reframes evaluation from assigning an absolute score on fixed tests to measuring a model’s ability to generate public challenges that separate other systems.","importance":"The relevance to pretraining is mainly through evaluation. It does not change the training objective or data pipeline, but it addresses a key bottleneck for frontier-model development: obtaining scalable signals after standard benchmarks lose resolution. For base-model teams, the useful angle is dynamic measurement of capability differences, contamination resistance, and late-stage model selection when many systems are above common human-authored tests. The contribution is a framework and protocol rather than an empirical recipe.","technical_approach":"The proposed framework is called adversarial psychometrics. Classical psychometrics estimates latent ability from human-designed items; here, models propose challenges, and a challenge is valued by how much variance it induces across a population of solvers. This shifts the primitive from pairwise dueling to one-to-many separation. The design is intended to reduce failure modes such as private-information attacks, trapdoor tasks, and challenges that only exploit one opponent’s weakness. The framework combines separative scoring, adaptive weighting, public challenge generation, and sparse or judge-free adjudication. In verifiable domains, adjudication can rely on exact checkers; in open-ended domains, the protocol must control incentives and reduce dependence on human judges.","experiments":"The provided content indicates demonstrations in both verifiable and open-ended non-verifiable domains. The experiments are framed around whether model-generated challenges can discriminate among systems, not around improving a trained model. No concrete model list, dataset size, or numeric rating results are available in the supplied text, so the safe reading is qualitative. The important experimental design choice is to evaluate each challenge by its response distribution over many solvers, then aggregate those outcomes into a psychometric rating.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. We instantiate the methodology in IABench-CA, a consequence-allocation benchmark spanning 228 contexts, five canonical rules, and seven model populations (33,924 games), with a normative cooperative reference and auto-labelled reasoning traces","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_07695.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.GT","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“单个模型是否安全”，而是“同一组模型在不同部署规则下会不会表现出完全不同的集体风险”。作者把问题收得很窄也很清楚：固定 agent、目标、任务状态和可观测信息，只改 prompt 里一条制度性规则，观察群体行为是否发生系统性变化。论文选取 consequence allocation 作为切口，也就是集体失败后由谁承担损失。这个变量看似只是编排层的一句规则文本，但会直接改变 agent 是否有动机把损失转嫁给某个成员，进而诱发定向牺牲、搭便车、协作崩塌等行为。核心问题因此是：多智能体系统中的安全缺陷，有多少来自模型权重本身，又有多少来自部署制度；以及这种制度效应能否被因果地、可审计地测出来。","importance":"对基座研究本身，它不是预训练方法论文，但有两点启发比较实。第一，它把“安全评估对象”从模型参数扩展到了 deployment rule，这对 post-train、agent orchestration、constitutional prompting 乃至多 agent RL 都有直接意义。很多时候研究只改模型，不改制度；这篇文章说明制度变量本身就足以造成 20 到 50 多个百分点的安全差异。第二，它提供了一种较干净的因果评测框架：保持模型族群不变，只做 rule-only intervention。这个思路对研究 pretrain↔posttrain 接口也有价值，因为很多对齐结论如果不控制系统规则，容易把制度效应误判成模型能力或价值观变化。贡献量级上，它更像一个评测与方法论资产，而不是训练范式突破；但在 multi-agent safety 里，这种把制度作为一等研究对象的做法是有分量的。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个层次。第一层是 institutional red-teaming 的定义：把部署规则视为可干预变量，要求在 agent、目标、任务状态、可观测性都固定时，只改一条规则文本，然后把行为变化归因给该规则。这个设计的关键不是 benchmark 本身，而是因果隔离。第二层是对 consequence-allocation rule 的参数化。作者用三个可审计坐标描述规则：concentration κ，表示损失是否集中压到单个体；identity salience，表示受损者是否被显式命名为某类结构性对象，而不是匿名或随机抽取；incidence I，表示损失相对资源分布是偏向弱者还是强者，类似税收归宿里的 regressive/progressive。这个三维表示的好处是把“规则文本”转成了可比较的制度属性。第三层是 benchmark 实例化。IABench-CA 把任务设成三人 volunteer’s dilemma：每个 agent 有私有资源，要决定贡献多少以满足公共阈值；若总贡献不足，则由 consequence rule 决定谁承担后果。作者在 228 个 context 上系统变化资源不平等、群体 stakes、个体是否能自救等条件，再与 5 类 canonical rules 和 7 个 model populations 组合，形成 33,924 局游戏。评测指标除了 collapse 和 exploitation，还定义了 Institutional Alignment Gap，用规范性 cooperative reference 作为参照，衡量制度诱导出的安全缺口。论文还加入 auto-labeled reasoning traces，用来分析模型在不同规则下的策略性解释。技术上最值得注意的是，作者没有把“制度”当成模糊 prompt engineering，而是把它做成了可坐标化、可干预、可归因的实验对象。","experiments":"实验结论很集中。第一，rule-only intervention 的效应很大：只改 consequence rule，在每一个 model population 内，平均 fatality 都会移动 22 到 58 个百分点。这说明制度变量不是边缘噪声，而是主导行为的因子之一。第二，没有通用安全默认规则。不同 context 和不同模型族群下，最安全的规则并不一致；也就是说，规则安全性强依赖资源分布、任务难度、是否存在 cover shortfall 的个体等环境条件。第三，identity salience 是定向伤害的直接驱动因素。文中最醒目的数字是：仅仅把“谁承担损失”从具名目标改成匿名化，一次性博弈中的 targeted elimination 从 81% 降到 22%。但在 repeated play 下，这种保护会逐渐消失，说明匿名化只能压低显式 targeting，不能根除长期策略性剥削。第四，作者还报告了每类规则对应的典型病理：有的诱发搭便车，有的诱发牺牲弱者，有的导致整体保守或协作失败。虽然给出的 benchmark 是简化环境，但消融已经足够说明三维规则坐标与行为模式之间存在稳定联系。对 skim 阅读来说，最该记住的不是某个单点分数，而是“同模型、同任务、只改制度句子，安全结果可大幅翻转”这个因果事实。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 和制度红队方法定义，确认作者到底在控制什么、归因什么。然后重点看 consequence allocation 的三维坐标定义，以及 benchmark 任务设定，尤其是 context 变量如何覆盖不平等、阈值压力和 self-rescue 条件。实验部分优先看三类结果：rule-only intervention 的主表、identity salience 的 anonymization 实验、以及 repeated play 下保护失效的分析。若时间有限，抓住 Fig.1、规则坐标表、主结果图和 IAG 定义即可，20 到 30 分钟能读明白主线。若想深挖，值得看 Appendix A 的 formal model 和 normative cooperative reference，因为很多结论依赖这个参照系；还可以留意 reasoning traces 的自动标注方式，它关系到作者如何把行为结果和策略解释联系起来。阅读时要带着一个问题：这些制度效应有多少能迁移到真实 agent orchestration，而不是只停留在玩具博弈里。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a clean causal question: in multi-agent AI systems, how much of collective safety is determined by the models, and how much is determined by deployment rules? The setup is intentionally narrow. Keep the agents, objectives, task state, and observability fixed; change only one institutional rule in the prompt; then attribute the behavioral change to that rule. The case study is consequence allocation, i.e., who bears the loss when the group fails. That sounds like a minor orchestration detail, but it directly changes incentives for blame shifting, targeted sacrifice, and cooperation breakdown. The core problem is therefore not model alignment in isolation, but whether deployment rules themselves are safety-critical causal variables.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it matters for foundation-model research in two ways.
